## 2026-10-16 20:58 Head of QA
- S-2 zero-writes: save keys, .bak and eggfarm.ui must not be written and must stay byte-identical. Only eggfarm.lease and telemetry may change, and they must carry no save fields.
- SD5-1: the shared guard script is updated to the final rule (persisted copy, zero tolerance; live state, 1 tick). The older persisted-1-tick allowance is removed. No private fork.
- 21:10 ZW refinement: telemetry payload fields (farm_level, coinsBefore) are allowed. ZW-5 fails only if an allowed key holds a serialized save or .bak object. eggfarm.tab is allowed like the lease. eggfarm.install_id may be created only if absent; any change or deletion of an existing value fails.
- 21:42 PR #1 @ 96e895b: CHANGES REQUESTED (7 Must). Export save ships as a separate PR before S-2, and ZW-2 flips to PASS on it. On a partial backup failure, the aborted attempt removes its own backup and the retry writes a fresh one. M4-A 677317d smoke PASS with Minor M4A-S1 (Firefox font preload). The fixed backup-failure SHA needs a Saves QA delta check before Latest.

## 2026-10-17 00:05 — L30-19a ruling (Head of QA)
A nudge that never fires does not pass a gate that requires the nudge to be shown. L30-19a uses the first time a casual-no-workers player reaches F25 with 0 workers as the nudge event (matching runtime AC L30-19b). Economy's probe gives next-hire-affordable P95 20 s / worst 20 s, which passes ≤3 min. Conditional on PM Farm/HoP confirming in the spec that the L25 nudge is its own trigger, separate from coop-full; if not, L30-19 fails as a design gap. Pins retarget to late_curve 8c5a08a8…, CSV 3e3d61fd….

## 2026-10-17 00:50 PR #7 F-1 ruling (HoP)
F-1 (the seller15 bot gives identical results on all 30 seeds) does not block rc1. For rc1, AC7 is judged on its deterministic run against the band (edge inclusive, so L18 on the edge passes). The AC7 P95 tail is marked NOT VERIFIED and is not counted as a pass; Economy's 100-seed sim covers the tail risk. Tracked item TR-AC7-TAIL: post-rc1, Economy adds per-seed variance, and AC7 must be re-run with its tail verified before any later release that changes pacing numbers. Goes in the rc1 release notes. F-2 (seed-count assert plus a P95 rank unit test) is still required for the PR #7 approve.

## 2026-10-17 00:58 AC7 variance bot (HoP)
The variance bot spec (17877b41) is approved and becomes the AC7 standard: at least 50 distinct L18 times per 100 seeds; L18 P95 between 3% and 15% above P50; AC7 judged at P5/P95; the fixed bot (var=None) stays as a regression fixture only. Band edges are inclusive, so with-workers L20 is a zero-margin pass. AC7 counts as VERIFIED only once the TS bot port lands; that is not an rc1 blocker but is required before any later release that changes pacing (TR-AC7-TAIL). The ac7-l18-25 14.75 was a step time on pre-F1 costs at ce7d59a, not a gate problem: on m4cols with workers it is 4.5 min, a PASS. HoE accepted the cost bug, and the gate runs with workers.

## 2026-10-19 10:08 — CSR-17 schedule rule + CSR-AC-IDB1 (D-HOP-34, on HoQA proposal)
- CSR-17 (field-level repair): if its code isn't at a pushed full SHA by 17:15, it moves to M4.2 automatically. If it is pushed, QA tests it last. If it hasn't passed by 08:00 Oct 20, it comes out of M4.1 (flagged off or reverted).
- QA testability draft for CSR-17: GO WITH CONDITIONS (qa/csr/csr-17-gonogo.md). C1–C3 and the spec freeze by sha256 are due 12:00, or it's NO-GO. C4–C6 are due before 17:15.
- New baseline AC CSR-AC-IDB1: a corrupt IndexedDB-only save never starts a silent new game. The raw bytes are kept as corrupt.* in the same store, the recovery dialog shows with Export, and save_corrupt {store: idb} is emitted. Found by Saves QA in its v0.2.3 review.

- QA-R21-PW (HoO ruling, 07:53 Oct 30; logged by HoQA 08:00): Playwright's detached browser groups are approved as a rule 21 exception, on four conditions: (1) the step runs under lane-run with the watchdog; (2) the browser is closed only through Playwright's own close; (3) a watchdog stop halts the run and gives HoO a stop list, with no manual cleanup; (4) amendment: right after launch, the browser's own PID is recorded in the step's lane pidfile in lane-run format (PID STARTTIME BOOT_ID ISO-time cmd), so lane-kill can stop that group exactly. No other process may be signalled. HoQA implementation note: in playwright-core 1.64 the Browser type has no process() (only BrowserServer and ElectronApplication do). The harness must use browserType.launchServer() and connect(), read server.process().pid, and close with browser.close() then server.close(). HoO informed.

## QA-D-1110-TRACE10-G0 — trace10 hook patch (G-0/G-1) APPROVED with conditions (Tue 10 Nov 2026 00:15 CET)
Basis: engineering/design/trace10-harness-of-record.md sha256 9f375d5f80a6bc8e…, §2.2; HoP D-HOP-2026-11-10-03 (R-1). HoQA side of G-0. HoE's OK is still needed separately.
- APPROVE the 3 observe-only hooks (onTick/onRunnerDraw/onVariance) in tests/bot-m5.ts as the only harness edit. Conditions: (a) the diff is limited to tests/bot-m5.ts types + `opt.<hook>?.()` calls + the R0/R alias; src/, data/ and balance.json are untouched (`git diff --stat origin/rc/v5 -- src data` empty); (b) with hooks unset, every existing bot-m5 test plus the flag-0 goldens are byte-identical and the full vitest suite has 0 fail; (c) a new test proves R calls R0 exactly once per draw with the same values (hooked vs unhooked equal sequence), onVariance fires after construction and before the first method, and onTick receives clones (mutation has no effect, per the P3 pattern); (d) the new bot-m5.ts sha becomes the harness of record only via HoP's re-pin, at the pinned RC sha.
- The fallback static structural-zero proof is NOT accepted for PASS. Runner must be a measured explicit 0 (EC-2).
- Q-1: P1 (untraced parity) + P2 (traced-full vs traced-minimal) + P3 are ACCEPTED as the buy-order/RNG-consumption evidence. No further harness change is required.
- R-2: either a runtime read with a guard test or a read-only cursor() getter in tests/py-random.ts is acceptable (HoE's choice); a getter counts as part of the same patch and falls under the same conditions.
- R-3: card version of record is product/m5-gate-acs.md sha1 6269b126 (D-HOP-2026-11-10-02); the producer still hashes the card at run time into inputs.card_sha256.
- This is not a PL-AC5(iii) PASS claim and authorizes no 600-run; that stays a separate Eng+Architect+QA GO.

## QA-D-1110-PAUDUR + QA-D-1110-COLDRIFT (Tue 10 Nov 2026 00:20 CET)
- PAUDUR: worker_resumed.paused_duration_s resets after a reload because the pause start is held in memory and isn't saved (HoE, Saves 8ee3db1 Draft). This does NOT break PAU-AC8 (AC-P10..P12 don't use the field) or PAU-AC9 (the paused flag, energy and callLockoutS are saved). Accepting the value is PMC's telemetry ruling. QA condition: after a reload the field must be finite, ≥0 and deterministic, counted from load per the ruling, with one test, and the errata must state it. PAU-AC8 is re-graded at the exact head of the D1/D2 PR once it's Ready.
- COLDRIFT: PMC audit sha1 e328cef1 (rc/v5 f20b9183), 6 breeds drifts in Saves code. Severity: D2 (an invalid breeds root that is an array, number or string throws in breeds-hatch.ts:21 every tick) is CRITICAL. It blocks the RC: a corrupt save must never crash every tick. D1 (save_migrated short shape fails the schema) is MAJOR. D3–D6 are MINOR. These join OQ-2/3/7 as expected-FAIL until the #95 follow-up lands. Saves QA adds the D2 root-type cases (array, number, string, null) plus a one-tick no-throw check. N3 (tap/session_end/offline_grant v5 fields not built, ST-AC19/20) is tracked as an RC AC FAIL until built.
